import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.Rank
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.UnitaryGroup
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.Block
import Mathlib.Data.Complex.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.Complex.Basic
import Mathlib.Analysis.Complex.Order
import Mathlib.Analysis.RCLike.Basic

/-!
# Phase 5: Finite-Dimensional Unitary Bandwidth-Depth Tradeoff (`UnitaryBandwidthDepth.lean`)

Formalizes the finite-dimensional kinematic core of the Bekenstein–Nyquist Shock Worldline
Conjecture (Phase 1 & Phase 5):
1. Canonical block-partitioned index space `BlockIdx d (N - d) := Fin d ⊕ Fin (N - d)` and equivalence
   with `Fin N` via `finSumFinEquiv` and `Matrix.reindex`.
2. Orthogonal bandlimit projector `P_Lambda = Matrix.fromBlocks 1 0 0 0` onto the `d`-dimensional
   low-frequency Bekenstein subspace `V_Lambda`, with ultraviolet complement `I - P_Lambda = Matrix.fromBlocks 0 0 0 1`.
3. `IsElementaryClockGate`: formal predicate for an elementary unitary gate leaking amplitude from `V_Lambda`
   into at most a single 1D clock wire `c ∈ Fin (N - d)`, and proof that `IsElementaryClockGate → rank ≤ 1`.
4. Unitary Gram identities, forward/reverse leakage rank equality `rank(A) = rank(B) = r`, and exact
   commutator rank identity `rank([P_Lambda, U]) = rank(A) + rank(B) = 2r`.
5. Complete inductive proofs of `unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff` (isometric agreement) and
   `unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff_compression` (block-compression dilation agreement).
   - **Multi-Wire Gate Count vs. Single-Bond Angle Sub-Stepping:** In this module, `L.length ≥ r`
     bounds the total number of elementary rank-1 clock-coupling gates (`L.length`) by the
     **spatial leakage rank** `r = rank((1 - P) U_nl P)` across independent spatial modes/wires.
     At a single shock bond where `r = 1`, rank subadditivity alone gives `L.length ≥ 1`; the
     multi-step sequential sub-stepping `L.length ≥ ⌈Θ / ϑ_max⌉` at a single bond under a bounded
     per-step rotation angle `0 < ϑ ≤ ϑ_max` is formalized in `NyquistRotorGates.lean`
     (`givensRotor_list_prod_in_phase` and `single_bond_angle_substepping_bound`).
-/

noncomputable section

open Matrix Module
open scoped ComplexOrder

namespace BekensteinNyquist

/-- Canonical block-partitioned index type separating `d` low-frequency Bekenstein modes
    from `m = N - d` ultraviolet orthogonal modes. -/
abbrev BlockIdx (d m : ℕ) := Fin d ⊕ Fin m

/-- Equivalence reindexing a block-partitioned matrix on `Fin d ⊕ Fin (N - d)` to `Fin (d + (N - d))`
    via `finSumFinEquiv`. -/
def reindexToFin (d m : ℕ) (M : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ) :
    Matrix (Fin (d + m)) (Fin (d + m)) ℂ :=
  Matrix.reindex finSumFinEquiv finSumFinEquiv M

/-- Orthogonal bandlimit projector `P_Lambda = diag(I_d, 0_m)` constructed via `Matrix.fromBlocks`. -/
def bandlimitProj (d m : ℕ) : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ :=
  Matrix.fromBlocks (1 : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℂ) 0 0 (0 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℂ)

/-- Ultraviolet complement projector `I - P_Lambda = diag(0_d, I_m)` via `Matrix.fromBlocks`. -/
def uvProj (d m : ℕ) : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ :=
  Matrix.fromBlocks (0 : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℂ) 0 0 (1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℂ)

/-- `bandlimitProj` and `uvProj` sum to the identity matrix. -/
theorem bandlimitProj_add_uvProj (d m : ℕ) :
    bandlimitProj d m + uvProj d m = (1 : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ) := by
  unfold bandlimitProj uvProj
  rw [← Matrix.fromBlocks_one]
  simp [Matrix.fromBlocks_add]

/-- `uvProj` is literally `1 - bandlimitProj`. -/
theorem uvProj_eq_one_sub_bandlimitProj (d m : ℕ) :
    uvProj d m = 1 - bandlimitProj d m := by
  rw [← bandlimitProj_add_uvProj d m, add_sub_cancel_left]

/-- `bandlimitProj` is idempotent (`P^2 = P`). -/
theorem bandlimitProj_mul_self (d m : ℕ) :
    bandlimitProj d m * bandlimitProj d m = bandlimitProj d m := by
  unfold bandlimitProj
  simp [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]

/-- `bandlimitProj` is Hermitian (`Pᴴ = P`). -/
theorem bandlimitProj_conjTranspose (d m : ℕ) :
    (bandlimitProj d m)ᴴ = bandlimitProj d m := by
  unfold bandlimitProj
  simp [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose]

section GenericIndex

variable {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]

/-- Forward Nyquist leakage matrix `A = (1 - P) * U * P`. -/
def forwardLeakage (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  (1 - P) * U * P

/-- Reverse Nyquist leakage matrix `B = P * U * (1 - P)`. -/
def reverseLeakage (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  P * U * (1 - P)

/-- Matrix commutator `[P, U] = P * U - U * P`. -/
def commutator (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  P * U - U * P

/-- The commutator decomposes into `[P, U] = P * U * (1 - P) - (1 - P) * U * P = B - A`. -/
theorem commutator_eq_reverse_sub_forward (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) :
    commutator P U = reverseLeakage P U - forwardLeakage P U := by
  unfold commutator reverseLeakage forwardLeakage
  noncomm_ring

omit [DecidableEq n] in
/-- Subadditivity of matrix rank over `ℂ`: `rank(A + B) ≤ rank(A) + rank(B)`. -/
theorem matrix_rank_add_le (A B : Matrix n n ℂ) :
    (A + B).rank ≤ A.rank + B.rank := by
  dsimp [Matrix.rank]
  rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_add]
  have h1 := Submodule.finrank_mono (LinearMap.range_add_le A.mulVecLin B.mulVecLin)
  have h2 := Submodule.finrank_sup_add_finrank_inf_eq
    (LinearMap.range A.mulVecLin) (LinearMap.range B.mulVecLin)
  omega

/-- Helper: `(1 - P) * (1 - P) = 1 - P` when `P * P = P`. -/
theorem one_sub_idem {P : Matrix n n ℂ} (hP : P * P = P) :
    (1 - P) * (1 - P) = 1 - P := by
  calc
    (1 - P) * (1 - P) = 1 - P - P + P * P := by noncomm_ring
    _ = 1 - P - P + P := by rw [hP]
    _ = 1 - P := by abel

/-- One-sided leakage decomposition identity:
    `(1 - P) * (W * U) * P = ((1 - P) * W * P) * (P * U * P) + ((1 - P) * W * (1 - P)) * ((1 - P) * U * P)`. -/
theorem one_sided_leakage_decomposition
    (P W U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    forwardLeakage P (W * U) =
      (forwardLeakage P W) * (P * U * P) +
      ((1 - P) * W * (1 - P)) * (forwardLeakage P U) := by
  have hQ := one_sub_idem hP
  unfold forwardLeakage
  calc
    (1 - P) * (W * U) * P
      = (1 - P) * W * 1 * U * P := by simp [Matrix.mul_assoc]
    _ = (1 - P) * W * (P * P + (1 - P) * (1 - P)) * U * P := by
      rw [hP, hQ, add_sub_cancel]
    _ = ((1 - P) * W * P) * (P * U * P) + ((1 - P) * W * (1 - P)) * ((1 - P) * U * P) := by
      noncomm_ring

/-- Single-step rank subadditivity for the one-sided leakage decomposition. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_mul_rank_le
    (P W U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    (forwardLeakage P (W * U)).rank ≤
      (forwardLeakage P W).rank + (forwardLeakage P U).rank := by
  rw [one_sided_leakage_decomposition P W U hP]
  refine le_trans (matrix_rank_add_le _ _) ?_
  exact add_le_add (Matrix.rank_mul_le_left _ _) (Matrix.rank_mul_le_right _ _)

/-- **Mori–Zwanzig 2-Step Markovian Prediction Defect Identity**:
    For any idempotent compression projector `P * P = P` and evolution operators `W, U`,
    the non-Markovian prediction defect `P * (W * U) * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P)`
    equals the product of the reverse leakage of `W` and the forward leakage of `U`:
    `reverseLeakage P W * forwardLeakage P U = (P * W * (1 - P)) * ((1 - P) * U * P)`. -/
theorem mori_zwanzig_markov_defect_eq_reverse_mul_forward
    (P W U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    P * (W * U) * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P) =
      reverseLeakage P W * forwardLeakage P U := by
  have hQ := one_sub_idem hP
  unfold reverseLeakage forwardLeakage
  calc
    P * (W * U) * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P)
      = P * W * 1 * U * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P) := by simp [Matrix.mul_assoc]
    _ = P * W * (P * P + (1 - P) * (1 - P)) * U * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P) := by
      rw [hP, hQ, add_sub_cancel]
    _ = (P * W * (1 - P)) * ((1 - P) * U * P) := by
      noncomm_ring

/-- **Mori–Zwanzig Memory Rank Bound**:
    The rank of the 2-step non-Markovian prediction defect is bounded above by both
    the forward Stinespring leakage rank of `U` and the reverse leakage rank of `W`. -/
theorem mori_zwanzig_markov_defect_rank_le
    (P W U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    (P * (W * U) * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P)).rank ≤ (forwardLeakage P U).rank ∧
    (P * (W * U) * P - (P * W * P) * (P * U * P)).rank ≤ (reverseLeakage P W).rank := by
  rw [mori_zwanzig_markov_defect_eq_reverse_mul_forward P W U hP]
  exact ⟨Matrix.rank_mul_le_right _ _, Matrix.rank_mul_le_left _ _⟩

/-! ### Commutator Rank Additivity and Unitary Gram / Dilation Identities -/

/-- Exact range decomposition of the commutator `[P, U] = B - A`:
    `range([P, U]) = range(A) ⊔ range(B)`. -/
theorem commutator_range_eq_sup (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    LinearMap.range (commutator P U).mulVecLin =
      LinearMap.range (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin ⊔
      LinearMap.range (reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · rintro y ⟨x, rfl⟩
    have hsplit : (commutator P U).mulVecLin x =
        (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin (-x) + (reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin x := by
      ext i
      rw [commutator_eq_reverse_sub_forward]
      simp [Matrix.mulVec_neg, sub_eq_neg_add]
    rw [hsplit]
    refine Submodule.add_mem_sup ?_ ?_
    · exact ⟨-x, rfl⟩
    · exact ⟨x, rfl⟩
  · refine sup_le ?_ ?_
    · rintro y ⟨x, rfl⟩
      have hCP : (commutator P U) * (-P) = forwardLeakage P U := by
        unfold commutator forwardLeakage
        calc
          (P * U - U * P) * (-P) = -P * U * P + U * (P * P) := by noncomm_ring
          _ = -P * U * P + U * P := by rw [hP]
          _ = (1 - P) * U * P := by noncomm_ring
      refine ⟨(-P).mulVecLin x, ?_⟩
      rw [← LinearMap.comp_apply, ← Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, hCP]
    · rintro y ⟨x, rfl⟩
      have hCQ : (commutator P U) * (1 - P) = reverseLeakage P U := by
        unfold commutator reverseLeakage
        calc
          (P * U - U * P) * (1 - P) = P * U * (1 - P) - U * (P - P * P) := by noncomm_ring
          _ = P * U * (1 - P) - U * (P - P) := by rw [hP]
          _ = P * U * (1 - P) := by noncomm_ring
      refine ⟨(1 - P).mulVecLin x, ?_⟩
      rw [← LinearMap.comp_apply, ← Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, hCQ]

/-- Orthogonal image intersection: `range(A) ⊓ range(B) = ⊥` when `P * P = P`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_inf_reverseLeakage_range_eq_bot (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    LinearMap.range (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin ⊓
      LinearMap.range (reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin = ⊥ := by
  rw [eq_bot_iff]
  rintro y ⟨⟨xA, hxA⟩, ⟨xB, hxB⟩⟩
  rw [Submodule.mem_bot]
  have hPA : P * forwardLeakage P U = 0 := by
    unfold forwardLeakage
    calc
      P * ((1 - P) * U * P) = (P - P * P) * U * P := by noncomm_ring
      _ = (P - P) * U * P := by rw [hP]
      _ = 0 := by noncomm_ring
  have hPB : P * reverseLeakage P U = reverseLeakage P U := by
    unfold reverseLeakage
    calc
      P * (P * U * (1 - P)) = (P * P) * U * (1 - P) := by noncomm_ring
      _ = P * U * (1 - P) := by rw [hP]
  calc
    y = (reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin xB := hxB.symm
    _ = (P * reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin xB := by rw [hPB]
    _ = P.mulVecLin ((reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin xB) := by
      rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, LinearMap.comp_apply]
    _ = P.mulVecLin y := by rw [hxB]
    _ = P.mulVecLin ((forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin xA) := by rw [← hxA]
    _ = (P * forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin xA := by
      rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, LinearMap.comp_apply]
    _ = 0 := by rw [hPA, Matrix.mulVecLin_zero, LinearMap.zero_apply]

/-- For any idempotent projector `P * P = P` and any matrix `U`, the rank of the commutator
    equals the sum of the forward and reverse leakage ranks:
    `rank([P, U]) = rank(A) + rank(B)`. -/
theorem commutator_rank_eq_add (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    (commutator P U).rank = (forwardLeakage P U).rank + (reverseLeakage P U).rank := by
  dsimp [Matrix.rank]
  rw [commutator_range_eq_sup P U hP]
  have hsup := Submodule.finrank_sup_add_finrank_inf_eq
    (LinearMap.range (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin)
    (LinearMap.range (reverseLeakage P U).mulVecLin)
  rw [forwardLeakage_inf_reverseLeakage_range_eq_bot P U hP, finrank_bot, add_zero] at hsup
  exact hsup

/-- Unitary off-diagonal Gram identity in projector form:
    `Aᴴ * A = P - (P * U * P)ᴴ * (P * U * P)` for any unitary `U` and Hermitian projector `P`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_conjTranspose_mul_self
    (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hP_star : Pᴴ = P) (hU : Uᴴ * U = 1) :
    (forwardLeakage P U)ᴴ * (forwardLeakage P U) = P - (P * U * P)ᴴ * (P * U * P) := by
  have hQ := one_sub_idem hP
  have hQ_star : (1 - P)ᴴ = 1 - P := by rw [conjTranspose_sub, conjTranspose_one, hP_star]
  have hA_star : (forwardLeakage P U)ᴴ = P * Uᴴ * (1 - P) := by
    unfold forwardLeakage
    simp only [conjTranspose_mul, hP_star, hQ_star, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  have hPUP_star : (P * U * P)ᴴ = P * Uᴴ * P := by
    simp only [conjTranspose_mul, hP_star, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  rw [hA_star, hPUP_star]
  unfold forwardLeakage
  set W := Uᴴ
  calc
    P * W * (1 - P) * ((1 - P) * U * P)
      = P * W * ((1 - P) * (1 - P)) * U * P := by noncomm_ring
    _ = P * W * (1 - P) * U * P := by rw [hQ]
    _ = P * (W * U) * P - P * W * P * U * P := by noncomm_ring
    _ = P * 1 * P - P * W * (P * P) * U * P := by rw [hU, hP]
    _ = P * P - P * W * P * (P * U * P) := by noncomm_ring
    _ = P - P * W * P * (P * U * P) := by rw [hP]

/-- Kernel dimension inequality between `(1 - P) U P` and `(1 - P) Uᴴ P` when `Uᴴ U = 1`. -/
theorem finrank_ker_forwardLeakage_le
    (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hU : Uᴴ * U = 1) :
    Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin) ≤
      Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P Uᴴ).mulVecLin) := by
  have hQ := one_sub_idem hP
  let M : Matrix n n ℂ := U * P + (1 - P)
  let N : Matrix n n ℂ := Uᴴ * P + (1 - P)
  have hBM : ∀ x ∈ LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin,
      M.mulVecLin x ∈ LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P Uᴴ).mulVecLin := by
    intro x hx
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hx ⊢
    have hSplit : U * P = P * U * P + forwardLeakage P U := by
      unfold forwardLeakage
      noncomm_ring
    have hUP : (P * U * P).mulVecLin x = (U * P).mulVecLin x := by
      rw [hSplit, Matrix.mulVecLin_add, LinearMap.add_apply, hx, add_zero]
    have hProd : forwardLeakage P Uᴴ * M = (1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P * U * P) := by
      unfold forwardLeakage M
      calc
        (1 - P) * Uᴴ * P * (U * P + (1 - P))
          = (1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P * U * P) + (1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P - P * P) := by noncomm_ring
        _ = (1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P * U * P) + (1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P - P) := by rw [hP]
        _ = (1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P * U * P) := by noncomm_ring
    calc
      (forwardLeakage P Uᴴ).mulVecLin (M.mulVecLin x)
        = (forwardLeakage P Uᴴ * M).mulVecLin x := by
          rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, LinearMap.comp_apply]
      _ = ((1 - P) * Uᴴ * (P * U * P)).mulVecLin x := by rw [hProd]
      _ = ((1 - P) * Uᴴ).mulVecLin ((P * U * P).mulVecLin x) := by
        rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, LinearMap.comp_apply]
      _ = ((1 - P) * Uᴴ).mulVecLin ((U * P).mulVecLin x) := by rw [hUP]
      _ = ((1 - P) * (Uᴴ * U) * P).mulVecLin x := by
        rw [← LinearMap.comp_apply, ← Matrix.mulVecLin_mul]
        congr 1
        noncomm_ring
      _ = ((1 - P) * P).mulVecLin x := by rw [hU, Matrix.mul_one]
      _ = 0 := by
        have hQP : (1 - P) * P = 0 := by
          rw [Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.one_mul, hP, sub_self]
        rw [hQP, Matrix.mulVecLin_zero, LinearMap.zero_apply]
  have hNM : ∀ x ∈ LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin,
      N.mulVecLin (M.mulVecLin x) = x := by
    intro x hx
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hx
    have hQUP : ((1 - P) * U * P).mulVecLin x = 0 := hx
    have hId : N * M = 1 + (1 - Uᴴ) * ((1 - P) * U * P) := by
      dsimp [M, N]
      calc
        (Uᴴ * P + (1 - P)) * (U * P + (1 - P))
          = Uᴴ * (P * U * P) + Uᴴ * (P - P * P) + (1 - P) * U * P + (1 - P) * (1 - P) := by
            noncomm_ring
        _ = Uᴴ * (U * P - (1 - P) * U * P) + Uᴴ * (P - P) + (1 - P) * U * P + (1 - P) := by
          rw [hP, hQ]
          congr 3
          noncomm_ring
        _ = (Uᴴ * U) * P + (1 - P) + (1 - Uᴴ) * ((1 - P) * U * P) := by noncomm_ring
        _ = 1 + (1 - Uᴴ) * ((1 - P) * U * P) := by
          rw [hU, Matrix.one_mul, add_sub_cancel]
    calc
      N.mulVecLin (M.mulVecLin x)
        = (N * M).mulVecLin x := by rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, LinearMap.comp_apply]
      _ = (1 + (1 - Uᴴ) * ((1 - P) * U * P)).mulVecLin x := by rw [hId]
      _ = x + (1 - Uᴴ).mulVecLin (((1 - P) * U * P).mulVecLin x) := by
        rw [Matrix.mulVecLin_add, LinearMap.add_apply, Matrix.mulVecLin_one, LinearMap.id_apply,
          Matrix.mulVecLin_mul, LinearMap.comp_apply]
      _ = x := by rw [hQUP, LinearMap.map_zero, add_zero]
  let f : LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P U).mulVecLin →ₗ[ℂ]
      LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P Uᴴ).mulVecLin :=
    { toFun := fun x => ⟨M.mulVecLin x.1, hBM x.1 x.2⟩
      map_add' := fun x y => Subtype.ext (LinearMap.map_add M.mulVecLin x.1 y.1)
      map_smul' := fun c x => Subtype.ext (LinearMap.map_smul M.mulVecLin c x.1) }
  have hf_inj : Function.Injective f := by
    intro x y hxy
    apply Subtype.ext
    have hval : M.mulVecLin x.1 = M.mulVecLin y.1 := congrArg Subtype.val hxy
    calc
      x.1 = N.mulVecLin (M.mulVecLin x.1) := (hNM x.1 x.2).symm
      _ = N.mulVecLin (M.mulVecLin y.1) := by rw [hval]
      _ = y.1 := hNM y.1 y.2
  exact LinearMap.finrank_le_finrank_of_injective hf_inj

/-- For any unitary matrix `U ∈ unitaryGroup n ℂ` and any Hermitian projector `P`,
    the forward and reverse Nyquist leakage matrices have equal rank: `rank(A) = rank(B)`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_rank_eq_reverseLeakage_rank
    (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hP_star : Pᴴ = P)
    (U : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ) :
    (forwardLeakage P U.val).rank = (reverseLeakage P U.val).rank := by
  have hU_left : U.valᴴ * U.val = 1 := Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff'.mp U.prop
  have hU_right : U.val * U.valᴴ = 1 := Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff.mp U.prop
  have hU_right' : (U.valᴴ)ᴴ * U.valᴴ = 1 := by rwa [conjTranspose_conjTranspose]
  have hKer1 := finrank_ker_forwardLeakage_le P U.val hP hU_left
  have hKer2 := finrank_ker_forwardLeakage_le P U.valᴴ hP hU_right'
  rw [conjTranspose_conjTranspose] at hKer2
  have hKerEq : Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P U.val).mulVecLin) =
      Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage P U.valᴴ).mulVecLin) :=
    le_antisymm hKer1 hKer2
  have hRN1 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker (forwardLeakage P U.val).mulVecLin
  have hRN2 := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker (forwardLeakage P U.valᴴ).mulVecLin
  have hRankAdj : (forwardLeakage P U.val).rank = (forwardLeakage P U.valᴴ).rank := by
    dsimp [Matrix.rank]
    omega
  have hRevAdj : (reverseLeakage P U.val)ᴴ = forwardLeakage P U.valᴴ := by
    unfold reverseLeakage forwardLeakage
    have hQ_star : (1 - P)ᴴ = 1 - P := by rw [conjTranspose_sub, conjTranspose_one, hP_star]
    simp only [conjTranspose_mul, hP_star, hQ_star, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  rw [← Matrix.rank_conjTranspose (reverseLeakage P U.val), hRevAdj, hRankAdj]

/-- **Commutator Rank Identity (`rank([P, U]) = 2r`)**:
    For any unitary `U ∈ unitaryGroup n ℂ` and Hermitian projector `P`,
    `rank([P, U]) = 2 * rank((1 - P) U P)`. -/
theorem commutator_rank_eq_two_mul_forwardLeakage_rank
    (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hP_star : Pᴴ = P)
    (U : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ) :
    (commutator P U.val).rank = 2 * (forwardLeakage P U.val).rank := by
  rw [commutator_rank_eq_add P U.val hP,
    ← forwardLeakage_rank_eq_reverseLeakage_rank P hP hP_star U, two_mul]

/-- **Dilation Rank Invariance under Block Compression**:
    If two unitary matrices `U₁, U₂ ∈ unitaryGroup n ℂ` have the same low-frequency block
    compression `P * U₁ * P = P * U₂ * P`, then their forward Nyquist leakage matrices have
    identical rank: `rank((1 - P) U₁ P) = rank((1 - P) U₂ P)`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_rank_eq_of_compression_eq
    (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hP_star : Pᴴ = P)
    (U₁ U₂ : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)
    (hComp : P * U₁.val * P = P * U₂.val * P) :
    (forwardLeakage P U₁.val).rank = (forwardLeakage P U₂.val).rank := by
  have hU₁ : U₁.valᴴ * U₁.val = 1 := Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff'.mp U₁.prop
  have hU₂ : U₂.valᴴ * U₂.val = 1 := Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff'.mp U₂.prop
  rw [← Matrix.rank_conjTranspose_mul_self (forwardLeakage P U₁.val),
    ← Matrix.rank_conjTranspose_mul_self (forwardLeakage P U₂.val),
    forwardLeakage_conjTranspose_mul_self P U₁.val hP hP_star hU₁,
    forwardLeakage_conjTranspose_mul_self P U₂.val hP hP_star hU₂,
    hComp]

/-! ### Sequential Unitary Circuits and Inductive Bandwidth-Depth Tradeoff -/

/-- Sequential product of a list of unitary matrices `L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)`. -/
def circuitProduct (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  (L.map Subtype.val).prod

@[simp]
theorem circuitProduct_nil :
    circuitProduct ([] : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) = 1 := rfl

@[simp]
theorem circuitProduct_cons (W : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ) (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) :
    circuitProduct (W :: L) = W.val * circuitProduct L := rfl

/-- `circuitProduct L` is itself a member of `Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ`. -/
theorem circuitProduct_mem_unitaryGroup (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)) :
    circuitProduct L ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ := by
  induction L with
  | nil =>
    rw [circuitProduct_nil]
    exact Submonoid.one_mem (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)
  | cons W tail ih =>
    rw [circuitProduct_cons]
    exact Submonoid.mul_mem (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ) W.prop ih

/-- Base case: the identity unitary has zero forward leakage across an idempotent projector `P`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_one_rank_eq_zero (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) :
    (forwardLeakage P 1).rank = 0 := by
  have hzero : forwardLeakage P 1 = 0 := by
    unfold forwardLeakage
    calc
      (1 - P) * 1 * P = (1 - P) * P := by simp
      _ = P - P * P := by rw [Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
      _ = P - P := by rw [hP]
      _ = 0 := sub_self P
  rw [hzero, Matrix.rank_zero]

/-- Inductive leakage rank bound over any unitary circuit `L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)`:
    if every elementary gate `W ∈ L` has `rank((1 - P) W P) ≤ 1`, then
    `rank((1 - P) (circuitProduct L) P) ≤ L.length`. -/
theorem circuitProduct_forwardLeakage_rank_le_length
    (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P)
    (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ))
    (hGates : ∀ W ∈ L, (forwardLeakage P W.val).rank ≤ 1) :
    (forwardLeakage P (circuitProduct L)).rank ≤ L.length := by
  induction L with
  | nil =>
    simp [forwardLeakage_one_rank_eq_zero P hP]
  | cons W tail ih =>
    rw [circuitProduct_cons, List.length_cons]
    have hW : (forwardLeakage P W.val).rank ≤ 1 := hGates W (List.mem_cons_self)
    have hTail : ∀ W' ∈ tail, (forwardLeakage P W'.val).rank ≤ 1 :=
      fun W' hW' => hGates W' (List.mem_cons_of_mem W hW')
    calc
      (forwardLeakage P (W.val * circuitProduct tail)).rank
        ≤ (forwardLeakage P W.val).rank + (forwardLeakage P (circuitProduct tail)).rank :=
          forwardLeakage_mul_rank_le P W.val (circuitProduct tail) hP
      _ ≤ 1 + tail.length := add_le_add hW (ih hTail)
      _ = tail.length + 1 := Nat.add_comm 1 tail.length

/-- **Phase 5 Main Theorem (Isometric Form — `unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff`)**:
    If `circuitProduct L * P = U_nl.val * P` and every gate `W ∈ L` has `rank((1 - P) W P) ≤ 1`,
    then `D = L.length ≥ r = rank((1 - P) U_nl P)`. -/
theorem unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff
    (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P)
    (U_nl : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)
    (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ))
    (hGates : ∀ W ∈ L, (forwardLeakage P W.val).rank ≤ 1)
    (hAgree : circuitProduct L * P = U_nl.val * P) :
    (forwardLeakage P U_nl.val).rank ≤ L.length := by
  have hLeakEq : forwardLeakage P (circuitProduct L) = forwardLeakage P U_nl.val := by
    unfold forwardLeakage
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, hAgree, ← Matrix.mul_assoc]
  rw [← hLeakEq]
  exact circuitProduct_forwardLeakage_rank_le_length P hP L hGates

/-- **Phase 5 Stronger Block-Compression Dilation Theorem (`unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff_compression`)**:
    Even if `circuitProduct L` only matches the compressed low-frequency block
    `P * (circuitProduct L) * P = P * U_nl.val * P`, unitarity forces
    `rank((1 - P) (circuitProduct L) P) = rank((1 - P) U_nl P)`, so `L.length ≥ r` still holds!
    Note: this theorem bounds the total gate count `L.length` by the spatial leakage rank `r`.
    For single-bond sequential sub-stepping where `r = 1` and each step has bounded angle `ϑ ≤ ϑ_max`,
    see `single_bond_angle_substepping_bound` in `NyquistRotorGates.lean`. -/
theorem unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff_compression
    (P : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hP_star : Pᴴ = P)
    (U_nl : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ)
    (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ))
    (hGates : ∀ W ∈ L, (forwardLeakage P W.val).rank ≤ 1)
    (hComp : P * circuitProduct L * P = P * U_nl.val * P) :
    (forwardLeakage P U_nl.val).rank ≤ L.length := by
  let U_D : Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ := ⟨circuitProduct L, circuitProduct_mem_unitaryGroup L⟩
  have hRankEq : (forwardLeakage P U_D.val).rank = (forwardLeakage P U_nl.val).rank :=
    forwardLeakage_rank_eq_of_compression_eq P hP hP_star U_D U_nl hComp
  rw [← hRankEq]
  exact circuitProduct_forwardLeakage_rank_le_length P hP L hGates

end GenericIndex

/-! ### 1D Clock Wire Support Predicate & Rank-1 Bound on `BlockIdx d m` -/

/-- Predicate stating that `W` only leaks amplitude from `V_Lambda` into a single 1D ultraviolet
    clock register wire `c ∈ Fin m`: for all UV indices `i ≠ c`, row `Sum.inr i` of
    `forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W` vanishes. -/
def IsElementaryClockGate (d m : ℕ) (c : Fin m)
    (W : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ i : Fin m, i ≠ c → ∀ col : BlockIdx d m,
    forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W (Sum.inr i) col = 0

/-- For any matrix `W`, every low-frequency row `Sum.inl j` of `forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W`
    is identically zero because `1 - bandlimitProj d m = uvProj d m = fromBlocks 0 0 0 1`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_bandlimitProj_inl_eq_zero
    (d m : ℕ) (W : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ) (j : Fin d) (col : BlockIdx d m) :
    forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W (Sum.inl j) col = 0 := by
  unfold forwardLeakage
  rw [← uvProj_eq_one_sub_bandlimitProj d m, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  unfold uvProj
  simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₁₁, Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₁₂]

/-- If `W` satisfies `IsElementaryClockGate d m c W`, then `forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W`
    factors as a rank-1 outer product `Matrix.vecMulVec` supported on wire `Sum.inr c`. -/
theorem forwardLeakage_eq_vecMulVec_of_isElementaryClockGate
    (d m : ℕ) (c : Fin m) (W : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ)
    (hGate : IsElementaryClockGate d m c W) :
    forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W =
      Matrix.vecMulVec
        (fun r : BlockIdx d m => if r = Sum.inr c then (1 : ℂ) else 0)
        (fun col : BlockIdx d m => forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W (Sum.inr c) col) := by
  ext r col
  rw [Matrix.vecMulVec_apply]
  cases r with
  | inl j =>
    have hne : (Sum.inl j : BlockIdx d m) ≠ Sum.inr c := Sum.inl_ne_inr
    rw [if_neg hne, zero_mul, forwardLeakage_bandlimitProj_inl_eq_zero]
  | inr i =>
    by_cases hic : i = c
    · subst hic
      rw [if_pos rfl, one_mul]
    · have hne : (Sum.inr i : BlockIdx d m) ≠ Sum.inr c := fun h => hic (Sum.inr.inj h)
      rw [if_neg hne, zero_mul, hGate i hic col]

/-- **1D Clock Wire Rank-1 Theorem**:
    Any matrix `W` satisfying `IsElementaryClockGate d m c W` has forward Nyquist leakage rank `≤ 1`. -/
theorem rank_forwardLeakage_le_one_of_isElementaryClockGate
    (d m : ℕ) (c : Fin m) (W : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ)
    (hGate : IsElementaryClockGate d m c W) :
    (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W).rank ≤ 1 := by
  rw [forwardLeakage_eq_vecMulVec_of_isElementaryClockGate d m c W hGate]
  exact Matrix.rank_vecMulVec_le _ _

/-- Specialization of `unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff` to `BlockIdx d (N - d)` where each gate
    satisfies the structural 1D wire predicate `IsElementaryClockGate`. -/
theorem unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff_clock_wires
    (N d : ℕ)
    (U_nl : Matrix.unitaryGroup (BlockIdx d (N - d)) ℂ)
    (L : List (Matrix.unitaryGroup (BlockIdx d (N - d)) ℂ))
    (hWires : ∀ W ∈ L, ∃ c : Fin (N - d), IsElementaryClockGate d (N - d) c W.val)
    (hComp : bandlimitProj d (N - d) * circuitProduct L * bandlimitProj d (N - d) =
             bandlimitProj d (N - d) * U_nl.val * bandlimitProj d (N - d)) :
    (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d (N - d)) U_nl.val).rank ≤ L.length := by
  refine unitary_bandwidth_depth_tradeoff_compression
    (bandlimitProj d (N - d))
    (bandlimitProj_mul_self d (N - d))
    (bandlimitProj_conjTranspose d (N - d))
    U_nl L ?_ hComp
  intro W hW
  rcases hWires W hW with ⟨c, hc⟩
  exact rank_forwardLeakage_le_one_of_isElementaryClockGate d (N - d) c W.val hc

/-- Rank invariance under `reindexToFin` (`Matrix.reindex finSumFinEquiv finSumFinEquiv`). -/
theorem reindexToFin_rank_eq (d m : ℕ) (M : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ) :
    (reindexToFin d m M).rank = M.rank :=
  Matrix.rank_reindex finSumFinEquiv finSumFinEquiv M

end BekensteinNyquist

=== FILE: NyquistRotorGates.lean ===

import UnitaryBandwidthDepth
import Mathlib.Analysis.SpecialFunctions.Trigonometric.Basic

/-!
# Phase A (Extensions 2 & 3A): SU(2) Givens Rotors, π-Rotor Negation, Two-Level Rabi Saturation & Tripartite Haar Routing (`NyquistRotorGates.lean`)

Extends `UnitaryBandwidthDepth.lean` with machine-checked formal proofs for:
1. **2×2 Complex-Phase SU(2) Givens Rotors on `BlockIdx 1 1` (`givensRotor`) & Multi-Wire Embedding (`embeddedGivensRotor`)**:
   - Defined directly on `BlockIdx 1 1 = Fin 1 ⊕ Fin 1` (`Sum.inl 0` = spatial UV mode `u_UV ∈ V_Λ`,
     `Sum.inr 0` = orthogonal clock wire `c ∈ V_Λ^⊥`) via `Matrix.fromBlocks`, and embedded into
     arbitrary `BlockIdx d m` (`s : Fin d, c : Fin m`) via `embeddedGivensRotor`.
   - Exact unitarity `givensRotor_mem_unitaryGroup` (`Gᴴ * G = 1` and `G * Gᴴ = 1`).
   - Direct compatibility with Phase 5's `IsElementaryClockGate` both on `BlockIdx 1 1`
     (`givensRotor_isElementaryClockGate`) and non-vacuously for arbitrary `d, m` on `BlockIdx d m`
     (`embeddedGivensRotor_isElementaryClockGate`).
   - Constructive in-phase clock-mass creation (`Δϕ = 0`, `ζ = 1`):
     `givensRotor ϑ₂ 1 * givensRotor ϑ₁ 1 = givensRotor (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) 1`, inductive multi-step
     composition `givensRotor_list_prod_in_phase`, and the single-bond sub-stepping bound
     `single_bond_angle_substepping_bound` (`Θ ≤ L.length * ϑ_max`).
   - Coherent anti-phase clock-mass annihilation / logical negation (`¬`) gate (`Δϕ = π`, `ζ = -1`):
     `givensRotor ϑ (-1) * givensRotor ϑ 1 = 1` (breaking dissipative monotonicity).
   - Shock Mach–Zehnder / Ramsey interference fringe formula (`givensRotor_ramsey_fringe_entry`):
     the net UV-to-clock forward leakage after two pulses is `(1 + ζ) sin ϑ cos ϑ`.
2. **Two-Level Rabi Saturation & Rank-1 Channel Capacity Bottleneck**:
   - `rabi_overrotation_reflection` (alias `pauli_saturation_reflection`): When a 2×2 clock wire is
     driven to full inversion at `ϑ = π / 2` (`sin(π/2) = 1, cos(π/2) = 0`), a second in-phase
     `π / 2` pulse over-rotates the two-level system back to `2 * (π / 2) = π` (`forwardLeakage = 0`),
     reflecting the amplitude back into the spatial channel with phase `-1`.
   - `rank_one_channel_ker_finrank` (alias `pauli_exclusion_blockade_ker_finrank`): By
     `rank_forwardLeakage_le_one_of_isElementaryClockGate` and rank-nullity, any elementary 1D clock
     gate `IsElementaryClockGate d m c W` has `finrank (ker (forwardLeakage P W)) ≥ d + m - 1`,
     forbidding simultaneous compression of two independent spatial UV modes into a single clock wire.
3. **Milestone 4 Tripartite 3-Mode Haar Router `(ū_b, u_{b,UV}, c_slot)` (`TripartiteIdx`)**:
   - Formalizes the 3-mode subspace `Fin 1 ⊕ BlockIdx 1 1` (`ū_b ⊕ u_{b,UV} ⊕ c_slot`), proving
     exact unitarity of Stage 1 (`u_{b,UV} ↔ c_slot`), Stage 2 (`ū_b ↔ c_slot`), and their composite
     router, and proving `stage2MeanReadback_preserves_uv_mode` (zero UV spike excitation during readback).
4. **Direct-Sum Norm & Purity Defect Identity (`spatial_compression_add_clock_leakage_eq_proj`)**:
   - Proves `(P U P)ᴴ (P U P) + Aᴴ A = P`, establishing that apparent spatial norm dissipation
     is identically equal to the clock-fiber Gram matrix `Aᴴ A`.
-/

noncomputable section

open Matrix

namespace BekensteinNyquist

/-! ### 1. 1×1 Scalar Block Helpers & 2×2 SU(2) Givens Rotors on `BlockIdx 1 1` -/

/-- Embeds a complex scalar `z : ℂ` as a `1 × 1` matrix on `Fin 1`. -/
def scalarMat1 (z : ℂ) : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) ℂ :=
  fun _ _ => z

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_apply (z : ℂ) (i j : Fin 1) : scalarMat1 z i j = z := rfl

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_zero : scalarMat1 0 = 0 := by
  ext i j; rfl

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_one : scalarMat1 1 = 1 := by
  ext i j
  fin_cases i; fin_cases j
  rfl

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_add (a b : ℂ) : scalarMat1 a + scalarMat1 b = scalarMat1 (a + b) := by
  ext i j; rfl

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_sub (a b : ℂ) : scalarMat1 a - scalarMat1 b = scalarMat1 (a - b) := by
  ext i j; rfl

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_neg (a : ℂ) : -scalarMat1 a = scalarMat1 (-a) := by
  ext i j; rfl

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_mul (a b : ℂ) : scalarMat1 a * scalarMat1 b = scalarMat1 (a * b) := by
  ext i j
  simp [Matrix.mul_apply, scalarMat1]

@[simp]
theorem scalarMat1_conjTranspose (a : ℂ) : (scalarMat1 a)ᴴ = scalarMat1 (star a) := by
  ext i j; rfl

/-- Helper: `star (r : ℂ) = (r : ℂ)` for any real `r : ℝ`. -/
@[simp]
theorem star_ofReal (r : ℝ) : star (r : ℂ) = (r : ℂ) :=
  Complex.conj_ofReal r

/-- Fundamental complex-cast Pythagorean identity: `cos² ϑ + sin² ϑ = 1`. -/
theorem ofReal_cos_sq_add_sin_sq (ϑ : ℝ) :
    (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) = 1 := by
  norm_cast
  have h := Real.cos_sq_add_sin_sq ϑ
  linarith [sq (Real.cos ϑ), sq (Real.sin ϑ)]

/-- General 2×2 complex-phase Givens rotor matrix on `BlockIdx 1 1 = Fin 1 ⊕ Fin 1`,
    where `Sum.inl 0` is the spatial Nyquist mode `u_UV ∈ V_Λ`, `Sum.inr 0` is the
    orthogonal clock wire `c ∈ V_Λ^⊥`, `ϑ : ℝ` is the rotation angle, and `ζ : ℂ` (`star ζ * ζ = 1`)
    is the rotor phase factor `e^{iϕ}`:
    `G(ϑ, ζ) = fromBlocks (cos ϑ) (-star ζ * sin ϑ) (ζ * sin ϑ) (cos ϑ)`. -/
def givensRotor (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) : Matrix (BlockIdx 1 1) (BlockIdx 1 1) ℂ :=
  Matrix.fromBlocks
    (scalarMat1 (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ))
    (scalarMat1 (- star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)))
    (scalarMat1 (ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)))
    (scalarMat1 (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ))

/-- Left unitarity of `givensRotor ϑ ζ` when `star ζ * ζ = 1`: `(givensRotor ϑ ζ)ᴴ * givensRotor ϑ ζ = 1`. -/
theorem givensRotor_conjTranspose_mul_self (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) (hζ : star ζ * ζ = 1) :
    (givensRotor ϑ ζ)ᴴ * givensRotor ϑ ζ = 1 := by
  have hζ_comm : ζ * star ζ = 1 := mul_comm (star ζ) ζ ▸ hζ
  have htrig := ofReal_cos_sq_add_sin_sq ϑ
  unfold givensRotor
  rw [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
  simp only [scalarMat1_conjTranspose, star_ofReal, star_mul, star_neg, star_star,
    scalarMat1_mul, scalarMat1_add]
  have h11 : (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) +
      (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * star ζ * (ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) = 1 := by
    calc
      (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * star ζ * (ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ))
        = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (star ζ * ζ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by ring
      _ = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + 1 * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by rw [hζ]
      _ = 1 := by rw [one_mul, htrig]
  have h12 : (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (-star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) +
      (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * star ζ * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) = 0 := by ring
  have h21 : (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -ζ * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) +
      (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) = 0 := by ring
  have h22 : (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -ζ * (-star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) +
      (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) = 1 := by
    calc
      (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -ζ * (-star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) + (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ)
        = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (ζ * star ζ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by ring
      _ = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + 1 * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by rw [hζ_comm]
      _ = 1 := by rw [one_mul, htrig]
  rw [h11, h12, h21, h22, scalarMat1_one, scalarMat1_zero, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]

/-- Right unitarity of `givensRotor ϑ ζ` when `star ζ * ζ = 1`: `givensRotor ϑ ζ * (givensRotor ϑ ζ)ᴴ = 1`. -/
theorem givensRotor_mul_conjTranspose_self (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) (hζ : star ζ * ζ = 1) :
    givensRotor ϑ ζ * (givensRotor ϑ ζ)ᴴ = 1 := by
  have hζ_comm : ζ * star ζ = 1 := mul_comm (star ζ) ζ ▸ hζ
  have htrig := ofReal_cos_sq_add_sin_sq ϑ
  unfold givensRotor
  rw [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
  simp only [scalarMat1_conjTranspose, star_ofReal, star_mul, star_neg, star_star,
    scalarMat1_mul, scalarMat1_add]
  have h11 : (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) +
      (-star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -ζ) = 1 := by
    calc
      (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (-star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -ζ)
        = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (star ζ * ζ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by ring
      _ = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + 1 * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by rw [hζ]
      _ = 1 := by rw [one_mul, htrig]
  have h12 : (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * star ζ) +
      (-star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) = 0 := by ring
  have h21 : ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) +
      (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -ζ) = 0 := by ring
  have h22 : ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * star ζ) +
      (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) = 1 := by
    calc
      ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * star ζ) + (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ)
        = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (ζ * star ζ) * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by ring
      _ = (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + 1 * ((Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ)) := by rw [hζ_comm]
      _ = 1 := by rw [one_mul, htrig]
  rw [h11, h12, h21, h22, scalarMat1_one, scalarMat1_zero, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]

/-- **Unitarity of `givensRotor`**:
    For any `ϑ : ℝ` and `ζ : ℂ` with `star ζ * ζ = 1`, `givensRotor ϑ ζ ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup (BlockIdx 1 1) ℂ`. -/
theorem givensRotor_mem_unitaryGroup (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) (hζ : star ζ * ζ = 1) :
    givensRotor ϑ ζ ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup (BlockIdx 1 1) ℂ := by
  rw [Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff]
  exact givensRotor_mul_conjTranspose_self ϑ ζ hζ

/-- Every `givensRotor ϑ ζ` satisfies the Phase 5 `IsElementaryClockGate 1 1 0` predicate. -/
theorem givensRotor_isElementaryClockGate (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) :
    IsElementaryClockGate 1 1 0 (givensRotor ϑ ζ) := by
  intro i hi col
  fin_cases i
  exact False.elim (hi rfl)

/-- General multi-wire embedding of a 2×2 Givens rotor onto spatial mode `s : Fin d` and
    clock wire `c : Fin m` inside `BlockIdx d m = Fin d ⊕ Fin m` for arbitrary `d, m : ℕ`. -/
def embeddedGivensRotor (d m : ℕ) (s : Fin d) (c : Fin m) (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) :
    Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ :=
  Matrix.fromBlocks
    (Matrix.of fun i j : Fin d =>
      if i = s ∧ j = s then (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) else if i = j then 1 else 0)
    (Matrix.of fun i : Fin d => fun j : Fin m =>
      if i = s ∧ j = c then -star ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) else 0)
    (Matrix.of fun i : Fin m => fun j : Fin d =>
      if i = c ∧ j = s then ζ * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) else 0)
    (Matrix.of fun i j : Fin m =>
      if i = c ∧ j = c then (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) else if i = j then 1 else 0)

/-- **Non-Vacuous Multi-Wire `IsElementaryClockGate` Theorem (`embeddedGivensRotor_isElementaryClockGate`)**:
    For any spatial dimension `d`, clock-register dimension `m` (including `m ≥ 2`), spatial mode
    `s : Fin d`, and target clock wire `c : Fin m`, `embeddedGivensRotor d m s c ϑ ζ` satisfies
    `IsElementaryClockGate d m c`, because its UV-to-bandlimit block `W₂₁` vanishes on all rows `i ≠ c`. -/
theorem embeddedGivensRotor_isElementaryClockGate
    (d m : ℕ) (s : Fin d) (c : Fin m) (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) :
    IsElementaryClockGate d m c (embeddedGivensRotor d m s c ϑ ζ) := by
  intro i hi col
  unfold forwardLeakage embeddedGivensRotor
  rw [← uvProj_eq_one_sub_bandlimitProj d m]
  unfold uvProj bandlimitProj
  simp only [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply, Matrix.mul_zero, Matrix.zero_mul, add_zero, zero_add,
    Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_one]
  cases col with
  | inl j =>
    simp [Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₁, hi]
  | inr j =>
    simp [Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₂]

/-- Identity rotor at zero angle: `givensRotor 0 1 = 1`. -/
@[simp]
theorem givensRotor_zero : givensRotor 0 1 = 1 := by
  unfold givensRotor
  simp [Real.cos_zero, Real.sin_zero, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]

/-- **In-Phase Constructive Clock-Mass Creation (`Δϕ = 0`, `ζ = 1`)**:
    Two sequential in-phase Givens rotations add their angles coherently:
    `givensRotor ϑ₂ 1 * givensRotor ϑ₁ 1 = givensRotor (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) 1`. -/
theorem givensRotor_in_phase_mul (ϑ₁ ϑ₂ : ℝ) :
    givensRotor ϑ₂ 1 * givensRotor ϑ₁ 1 = givensRotor (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) 1 := by
  have h11 : (Real.cos ϑ₂ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ₁ : ℂ) + -(Real.sin ϑ₂ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ₁ : ℂ) =
      (Real.cos (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) : ℂ) := by
    norm_cast
    rw [Real.cos_add]
    ring
  have h12 : (Real.cos ϑ₂ : ℂ) * -(Real.sin ϑ₁ : ℂ) + -(Real.sin ϑ₂ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ₁ : ℂ) =
      -(Real.sin (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) : ℂ) := by
    norm_cast
    rw [Real.sin_add]
    ring
  have h21 : (Real.sin ϑ₂ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ₁ : ℂ) + (Real.cos ϑ₂ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ₁ : ℂ) =
      (Real.sin (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) : ℂ) := by
    norm_cast
    rw [Real.sin_add]
    ring
  have h22 : (Real.sin ϑ₂ : ℂ) * -(Real.sin ϑ₁ : ℂ) + (Real.cos ϑ₂ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ₁ : ℂ) =
      (Real.cos (ϑ₁ + ϑ₂) : ℂ) := by
    norm_cast
    rw [Real.cos_add]
    ring
  unfold givensRotor
  rw [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
  simp only [star_one, one_mul, neg_one_mul, scalarMat1_mul, scalarMat1_add, h11, h12, h21, h22]

/-- **In-Phase Angle Doubling (`G₀(ϑ) G₀(ϑ) = G₀(2ϑ)`)**:
    Special case of `givensRotor_in_phase_mul` for equal pulse angles `ϑ₁ = ϑ₂ = ϑ`. -/
theorem givensRotor_in_phase_double (ϑ : ℝ) :
    givensRotor ϑ 1 * givensRotor ϑ 1 = givensRotor (2 * ϑ) 1 := by
  rw [two_mul]
  exact givensRotor_in_phase_mul ϑ ϑ

/-- **Inductive Single-Bond In-Phase Sub-Stepping Product (`givensRotor_list_prod_in_phase`)**:
    Any sequential list `angles : List ℝ` of in-phase Givens rotations on a single shock bond
    composes to the single Givens rotation `givensRotor angles.sum 1` with total angle `angles.sum`. -/
theorem givensRotor_list_prod_in_phase (angles : List ℝ) :
    (angles.map (fun ϑ => givensRotor ϑ 1)).prod = givensRotor angles.sum 1 := by
  induction angles with
  | nil =>
    simp [givensRotor_zero]
  | cons ϑ tail ih =>
    rw [List.map_cons, List.prod_cons, ih, givensRotor_in_phase_mul, List.sum_cons, add_comm]

/-- Helper: If every step angle `ϑ ∈ angles` is bounded above by `ϑ_max`, then the cumulative
    rotation angle `angles.sum` is bounded above by `angles.length * ϑ_max`. -/
theorem list_sum_le_length_mul (angles : List ℝ) (ϑ_max : ℝ)
    (h_step : ∀ ϑ ∈ angles, ϑ ≤ ϑ_max) :
    angles.sum ≤ (angles.length : ℝ) * ϑ_max := by
  induction angles with
  | nil =>
    simp
  | cons a tail ih =>
    have ha : a ≤ ϑ_max := h_step a List.mem_cons_self
    have htail : tail.sum ≤ (tail.length : ℝ) * ϑ_max :=
      ih (fun ϑ hϑ => h_step ϑ (List.mem_cons_of_mem a hϑ))
    simp only [List.sum_cons, List.length_cons, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
    linarith

/-- **Single-Bond Sequential Sub-Stepping Lower Bound (`single_bond_angle_substepping_bound`)**:
    When a single shock bond must accumulate a target rotation angle `Θ` (`Θ ≤ angles.sum`) using
    elementary in-phase Givens gates whose individual rotation angle per clock tick is bounded by
    `ϑ ≤ ϑ_max`, the sequential gate count `angles.length` satisfies `Θ ≤ angles.length * ϑ_max`
    (i.e. `angles.length ≥ ⌈Θ / ϑ_max⌉`), complementing the multi-wire rank bound of
    `UnitaryBandwidthDepth.lean`. -/
theorem single_bond_angle_substepping_bound
    (angles : List ℝ) (Θ ϑ_max : ℝ)
    (h_step : ∀ ϑ ∈ angles, ϑ ≤ ϑ_max)
    (h_target : Θ ≤ angles.sum) :
    (angles.map (fun ϑ => givensRotor ϑ 1)).prod = givensRotor angles.sum 1 ∧
    Θ ≤ (angles.length : ℝ) * ϑ_max :=
  ⟨givensRotor_list_prod_in_phase angles,
   le_trans h_target (list_sum_le_length_mul angles ϑ_max h_step)⟩

/-- **Anti-Phase Coherent Clock-Mass Annihilation / Logical Negation (`¬`) Gate (`Δϕ = π`, `ζ = -1`)**:
    When a second shock arrives with relative rotor phase `Δϕ = π` (`ζ = e^{iπ} = -1`),
    `givensRotor ϑ (-1) * givensRotor ϑ 1 = 1`, discharging 100% of the clock state back into
    the spatial mode and realizing the non-monotone negation (`¬`) gate required to evade MPCVP. -/
theorem givensRotor_anti_phase_mul_eq_one (ϑ : ℝ) :
    givensRotor ϑ (-1) * givensRotor ϑ 1 = 1 := by
  have htrig := ofReal_cos_sq_add_sin_sq ϑ
  unfold givensRotor
  rw [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
  simp only [star_neg, star_one, neg_neg, one_mul, neg_one_mul, scalarMat1_mul, scalarMat1_add]
  have h11 : (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) = 1 := htrig
  have h12 : (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * -(Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) + (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) = 0 := by ring
  have h21 : -(Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) + (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) = 0 := by ring
  have h22 : -(Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * -(Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) + (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) = 1 := by
    linear_combination htrig
  rw [h11, h12, h21, h22, scalarMat1_one, scalarMat1_zero, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]

/-- **Shock Mach–Zehnder / Ramsey Interference Fringe Formula**:
    For any relative phase factor `ζ : ℂ`, the net UV-to-clock off-diagonal matrix element
    `(Sum.inr 0, Sum.inl 0)` of the two-pulse collision operator `givensRotor ϑ ζ * givensRotor ϑ 1`
    is `(1 + ζ) * sin ϑ * cos ϑ`. -/
theorem givensRotor_ramsey_fringe_entry (ϑ : ℝ) (ζ : ℂ) :
    (givensRotor ϑ ζ * givensRotor ϑ 1) (Sum.inr 0) (Sum.inl 0) =
      (1 + ζ) * (Real.sin ϑ : ℂ) * (Real.cos ϑ : ℂ) := by
  unfold givensRotor
  rw [Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
  simp only [star_one, scalarMat1_mul, scalarMat1_add, Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₁, scalarMat1_apply]
  ring

/-! ### 2. Two-Level Rabi Saturation & Rank-1 Channel Capacity Bottleneck -/

/-- **Two-Level Rabi Over-Rotation Reflection at `ϑ = π / 2` (`rabi_overrotation_reflection`)**:
    When a 2×2 clock wire is driven to full inversion by a `ϑ = π / 2` rotation, applying a
    second in-phase `π / 2` rotation (`givensRotor (π/2) 1 * givensRotor (π/2) 1 = givensRotor π 1`)
    over-rotates the two-level system back to zero net UV-to-clock transfer (`(Sum.inr 0, Sum.inl 0) = 0`)
    and reflects the amplitude back into the spatial channel with phase `-1`. -/
theorem rabi_overrotation_reflection :
    (givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1 * givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1) (Sum.inr 0) (Sum.inl 0) = 0 ∧
    (givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1 * givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1) (Sum.inl 0) (Sum.inl 0) = -1 := by
  rw [givensRotor_in_phase_mul]
  have hpi : Real.pi / 2 + Real.pi / 2 = Real.pi := add_halves Real.pi
  rw [hpi]
  unfold givensRotor
  simp [Real.sin_pi, Real.cos_pi]

/-- Backward-compatible alias for `rabi_overrotation_reflection`. -/
theorem pauli_saturation_reflection :
    (givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1 * givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1) (Sum.inr 0) (Sum.inl 0) = 0 ∧
    (givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1 * givensRotor (Real.pi / 2) 1) (Sum.inl 0) (Sum.inl 0) = -1 :=
  rabi_overrotation_reflection

/-- **Rank-1 Channel Capacity Bottleneck via Rank-Nullity on `BlockIdx d m` (`rank_one_channel_ker_finrank`)**:
    For any elementary 1D clock gate `W` satisfying `IsElementaryClockGate d m c W`,
    the forward leakage operator `forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W` has rank at most `1`,
    so its kernel has dimension at least `(d + m) - 1`:
    `d + m ≤ Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W).mulVecLin) + 1`.
    Consequently, at most a single 1D spatial channel can couple into wire `c` per elementary step. -/
theorem rank_one_channel_ker_finrank
    (d m : ℕ) (c : Fin m) (W : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ)
    (hGate : IsElementaryClockGate d m c W) :
    d + m ≤
      Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W).mulVecLin) + 1 := by
  have hRank : (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W).rank ≤ 1 :=
    rank_forwardLeakage_le_one_of_isElementaryClockGate d m c W hGate
  have hRN := LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker
    (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W).mulVecLin
  have hDim : Module.finrank ℂ (BlockIdx d m → ℂ) = d + m := by
    simp [BlockIdx, Fintype.card_sum, Fintype.card_fin]
  dsimp [Matrix.rank] at hRank
  omega

/-- Backward-compatible alias for `rank_one_channel_ker_finrank`. -/
theorem pauli_exclusion_blockade_ker_finrank
    (d m : ℕ) (c : Fin m) (W : Matrix (BlockIdx d m) (BlockIdx d m) ℂ)
    (hGate : IsElementaryClockGate d m c W) :
    d + m ≤
      Module.finrank ℂ (LinearMap.ker (forwardLeakage (bandlimitProj d m) W).mulVecLin) + 1 :=
  rank_one_channel_ker_finrank d m c W hGate

/-! ### 3. Milestone 4 Tripartite 3-Mode Haar Router `(ū_b, u_{b,UV}, c_slot)` -/

/-- Index type for the 3-mode Milestone 4 subspace:
    - `Sum.inl 0`: smooth local Haar mean mode `ū_b = (u_{b_0} + u_{b_1}) / √2`
    - `Sum.inr (Sum.inl 0)`: antisymmetric Nyquist difference mode `u_{b,UV} = (u_{b_0} - u_{b_1}) / √2`
    - `Sum.inr (Sum.inr 0)`: co-moving worldline clock slot `c_slot` -/
abbrev TripartiteIdx := Fin 1 ⊕ BlockIdx 1 1

/-- Stage 1 UV Stabilization gate on `TripartiteIdx` (Monograph Eq. 16),
    rotating `(u_{b,UV}, c_slot)` via `givensRotor ϑ₁ 1` while acting as the identity on `ū_b`. -/
def stage1UVStabilizer (ϑ₁ : ℝ) : Matrix TripartiteIdx TripartiteIdx ℂ :=
  Matrix.fromBlocks 1 0 0 (givensRotor ϑ₁ 1)

/-- `stage1UVStabilizer ϑ₁` is strictly unitary (`∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup TripartiteIdx ℂ`). -/
theorem stage1UVStabilizer_mem_unitaryGroup (ϑ₁ : ℝ) :
    stage1UVStabilizer ϑ₁ ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup TripartiteIdx ℂ := by
  rw [Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff]
  change stage1UVStabilizer ϑ₁ * (stage1UVStabilizer ϑ₁)ᴴ = 1
  have hG : givensRotor ϑ₁ 1 * (givensRotor ϑ₁ 1)ᴴ = 1 :=
    givensRotor_mul_conjTranspose_self ϑ₁ 1 (by simp)
  unfold stage1UVStabilizer
  rw [Matrix.fromBlocks_conjTranspose, Matrix.fromBlocks_multiply]
  simp [hG, Matrix.fromBlocks_one]

/-- Stage 2 Coherent Collision Readback gate on `TripartiteIdx` (Monograph Eq. 17),
    rotating stored clock condensate `c_slot` (`Sum.inr (Sum.inr 0)`) into the smooth local mean
    mode `ū_b` (`Sum.inl 0`) by `θ_rec` while leaving `u_{b,UV}` (`Sum.inr (Sum.inl 0)`) untouched. -/
def stage2MeanReadback (θ_rec : ℝ) : Matrix TripartiteIdx TripartiteIdx ℂ
  | Sum.inl _, Sum.inl _ => (Real.cos θ_rec : ℂ)
  | Sum.inl _, Sum.inr (Sum.inr _) => (Real.sin θ_rec : ℂ)
  | Sum.inr (Sum.inl _), Sum.inr (Sum.inl _) => 1
  | Sum.inr (Sum.inr _), Sum.inl _ => -(Real.sin θ_rec : ℂ)
  | Sum.inr (Sum.inr _), Sum.inr (Sum.inr _) => (Real.cos θ_rec : ℂ)
  | _, _ => 0

/-- **Zero UV Spike Excitation During Stage 2 Readback**:
    Applying `stage2MeanReadback θ_rec` to any 3-mode state `v : TripartiteIdx → ℂ` leaves the
    antisymmetric Nyquist difference component `v (Sum.inr (Sum.inl 0))` (`u_{b,UV}`) completely
    unchanged, eliminating the negative-viscosity UV spike avalanche. -/
theorem stage2MeanReadback_preserves_uv_mode (θ_rec : ℝ) (v : TripartiteIdx → ℂ) :
    (stage2MeanReadback θ_rec).mulVec v (Sum.inr (Sum.inl 0)) = v (Sum.inr (Sum.inl 0)) := by
  simp [stage2MeanReadback, Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Fintype.sum_sum_type]

/-- `stage2MeanReadback θ_rec` is strictly unitary (`∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup TripartiteIdx ℂ`). -/
theorem stage2MeanReadback_mem_unitaryGroup (θ_rec : ℝ) :
    stage2MeanReadback θ_rec ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup TripartiteIdx ℂ := by
  have htrig := ofReal_cos_sq_add_sin_sq θ_rec
  have hcos_star : star (Real.cos θ_rec : ℂ) = (Real.cos θ_rec : ℂ) :=
    Complex.conj_ofReal (Real.cos θ_rec)
  have hsin_star : star (Real.sin θ_rec : ℂ) = (Real.sin θ_rec : ℂ) :=
    Complex.conj_ofReal (Real.sin θ_rec)
  rw [Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff]
  change stage2MeanReadback θ_rec * (stage2MeanReadback θ_rec)ᴴ = 1
  ext (i | (i | i)) (j | (j | j)) <;> fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.conjTranspose_apply, Fintype.sum_sum_type,
      Fin.sum_univ_one, stage2MeanReadback, star_neg, star_zero, star_one,
      hcos_star, hsin_star, Matrix.one_apply, reduceCtorEq,
      Sum.inr.injEq, ite_true, ite_false, mul_zero, zero_mul,
      add_zero, zero_add, mul_neg, neg_mul, neg_neg] <;>
    first | rfl | linear_combination htrig | ring

/-- **Full Milestone 4 Tripartite Collision Router** (`Stage 2 ∘ Stage 1`) on `(ū_b, u_{b,UV}, c_slot)`:
    First applies `stage1UVStabilizer ϑ₁` to rotate overflowing Nyquist difference modes into the
    worldline clock slot, then applies `stage2MeanReadback θ_rec` to rotate stored clock amplitude
    back into the smooth local Haar mean mode `ū_b`. -/
def milestone4TripartiteRouter (ϑ₁ θ_rec : ℝ) : Matrix TripartiteIdx TripartiteIdx ℂ :=
  stage2MeanReadback θ_rec * stage1UVStabilizer ϑ₁

/-- **Strict Unitarity of the Milestone 4 Tripartite Collision Router**:
    The composite 2-stage router `milestone4TripartiteRouter ϑ₁ θ_rec` belongs to
    `Matrix.unitaryGroup TripartiteIdx ℂ`, guaranteeing exact norm preservation
    `|ū_out|² + |u_{UV,out}|² + |c_out|² = |ū_in|² + |u_{UV,in}|² + |c_in|²`. -/
theorem milestone4TripartiteRouter_mem_unitaryGroup (ϑ₁ θ_rec : ℝ) :
    milestone4TripartiteRouter ϑ₁ θ_rec ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup TripartiteIdx ℂ :=
  Submonoid.mul_mem _ (stage2MeanReadback_mem_unitaryGroup θ_rec)
    (stage1UVStabilizer_mem_unitaryGroup ϑ₁)

/-! ### 4. Direct-Sum Spatial Norm Conservation & Apparent Shock Entropy Defect (Extension 3A) -/

/-- **Spatial Block-Compression + Clock-Leakage Gram Partition of Unity**:
    For any unitary `U` and Hermitian idempotent bandlimit projector `P`,
    `(P * U * P)ᴴ * (P * U * P) + (forwardLeakage P U)ᴴ * (forwardLeakage P U) = P`.
    This proves that any norm lost from the projected spatial flow `P U P` is stored
    without loss in the orthogonal worldline clock fiber `forwardLeakage P U = (1 - P) U P`. -/
theorem spatial_compression_add_clock_leakage_eq_proj
    {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]
    (P U : Matrix n n ℂ) (hP : P * P = P) (hP_star : Pᴴ = P) (hU : Uᴴ * U = 1) :
    (P * U * P)ᴴ * (P * U * P) + (forwardLeakage P U)ᴴ * (forwardLeakage P U) = P := by
  rw [forwardLeakage_conjTranspose_mul_self P U hP hP_star hU]
  abel

end BekensteinNyquist

import Mathlib.Data.Real.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp

/-!
# Phase A (Extension 1): Ginzburg–Landau Defect Condensate & Nyquist Horizon Criticality (`NyquistHiggsDuality.lean`)

Formalizes the localized Ginzburg–Landau shock-core defect condensate potential
`V_eff(|c|²) = σ₀ |c|² + λ |c|⁴` and its algebraic isomorphism with the quartic Higgs potential
(`Physlib.Particles.StandardModel.HiggsBoson.Potential`) across the Bekenstein–Nyquist
worldline clock fiber (Monograph Section 4.4.3, Equations 19–20, and Table 6):

- **Physical Interpretation (Localized Defect Condensate vs. Translation-Invariant Vacuum):**
  In the Bekenstein–Nyquist lattice, the bare saturation margin `σ₀(x,t) = 1 - F₀(x,t) / (Λ_Bek * ν_ML)`
  is positive (`σ₀ > 0`) throughout the smooth sub-Nyquist bulk (where the equilibrium clock
  register norm is zero, `|c_eq|² = 0`) and becomes negative (`σ₀ < 0`) strictly inside localized,
  over-steepened shock cores where nonlinear spectral flux exceeds `Λ_Bek * ν_ML`. Within a shock
  core, transferring norm `|c|²` into the clock register linearly relieves the spatial overload,
  generating a localized Ginzburg–Landau defect condensate `|c_eq|² = -σ₀ / (2λ) > 0` whose quartic
  free-energy polynomial is algebraically isomorphic to `StandardModel.HiggsField.Potential.toFun`
  under `μ² = -σ₀`.

1. `HiggsPotential`: Exact mirror of `StandardModel.HiggsField.Potential` in `Physlib`
   (`- P.μ2 * normSq + P.𝓵 * normSq * normSq`), along with `complete_square`,
   `pos_𝓵_lower_bound`, and `minimum_iff_normSq_eq`.
2. `toHiggsPotential`: Explicit sign-convention bridge `μ2 := -σ₀, 𝓵 := lam` mapping the
   Bekenstein–Nyquist Saturation Functional `σ₀(x,t) = 1 - F₀(x,t) / (Λ_Bek * ν_ML)` to
   `Physlib`'s quadratic mass parameter.
3. `saturation_linear_relaxation`: Formal proof that linear commutator defect absorption
   `F(|c|²) = F₀ - κ |c|²` shifts the saturation functional to `Σ(|c|²) = σ₀ + 2 λ |c|²`
   with `λ = κ / (2 Λ_Bek ν_ML)`.
4. `subNyquist_unique_minimum_zero`: In the sub-Nyquist bulk (`σ₀ > 0, λ > 0`),
   the unique global minimum on `c_normSq ≥ 0` is `c_normSq = 0` (`m_j = 0`).
5. `superNyquist_condensate_minimum` & `superNyquist_horizon_restoration`: Inside an overloaded
   super-Nyquist shock core (`σ₀ < 0, λ > 0`), the unexcited state `c = 0` relaxes to the strictly
   positive defect-condensate norm `c_eq² = -σ₀ / (2λ) > 0`, which restores the effective saturation
   margin to the critical Sonic/Nyquist Horizon `Σ(c_eq²) = σ₀ + 2λ c_eq² = 0`.
6. `quarticCoupling_cutoff_independent`: Proves that under Paley–Wiener linear scaling
   `κ = c₁ Λ_Bek` and `θ_Nyq = Λ_Bek ν_ML`, the quartic coupling `λ = c₁ / (2 ν_ML)`
   is strictly independent of the ultraviolet cutoff `Λ_Bek`.
-/

noncomputable section

namespace BekensteinNyquist

/-! ### 1. Exact Mirror of `Physlib.Particles.StandardModel.HiggsBoson.Potential` -/

/-- Mirror of `StandardModel.HiggsField.Potential` from `Physlib`:
    parameterized by `μ2 : ℝ` (with `-μ2` as the quadratic coefficient) and quartic coupling `𝓵 : ℝ`. -/
structure HiggsPotential where
  μ2 : ℝ
  𝓵 : ℝ

namespace HiggsPotential

/-- Evaluation of `HiggsPotential` on the squared norm `normSq = ‖φ‖² ≥ 0`:
    `V(normSq) = - P.μ2 * normSq + P.𝓵 * normSq * normSq`. -/
def toFun (P : HiggsPotential) (normSq : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  - P.μ2 * normSq + P.𝓵 * normSq * normSq

/-- Completion of the square for the Higgs potential (`Physlib` `Potential.complete_square`). -/
theorem complete_square (P : HiggsPotential) (h : P.𝓵 ≠ 0) (normSq : ℝ) :
    P.toFun normSq = P.𝓵 * (normSq - P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵)) ^ 2 - (P.μ2 ^ 2 / (4 * P.𝓵)) := by
  unfold toFun
  field_simp
  ring

/-- Global lower bound `- (μ2² / (4 𝓵)) ≤ P.toFun normSq` when `0 < P.𝓵`
    (`Physlib` `Potential.pos_𝓵_quadDiscrim_zero_bound`). -/
theorem pos_𝓵_lower_bound (P : HiggsPotential) (h : 0 < P.𝓵) (normSq : ℝ) :
    - (P.μ2 ^ 2 / (4 * P.𝓵)) ≤ P.toFun normSq := by
  rw [P.complete_square (ne_of_gt h) normSq]
  have hsq : 0 ≤ (normSq - P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵)) ^ 2 := sq_nonneg _
  have hmul : 0 ≤ P.𝓵 * (normSq - P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵)) ^ 2 := mul_nonneg (le_of_lt h) hsq
  linarith

/-- Characterization of the global minimum of `HiggsPotential` (`Physlib` `quadDiscrim_eq_zero_iff_normSq`):
    `P.toFun normSq = - (P.μ2² / (4 P.𝓵)) ↔ normSq = P.μ2 / (2 P.𝓵)`. -/
theorem minimum_iff_normSq_eq (P : HiggsPotential) (h : 0 < P.𝓵) (normSq : ℝ) :
    P.toFun normSq = - (P.μ2 ^ 2 / (4 * P.𝓵)) ↔ normSq = P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵) := by
  rw [P.complete_square (ne_of_gt h) normSq]
  constructor
  · intro hEq
    have hzero : P.𝓵 * (normSq - P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵)) ^ 2 = 0 := by linarith
    have hsq0 : (normSq - P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵)) ^ 2 = 0 :=
      (mul_eq_zero.mp hzero).resolve_left (ne_of_gt h)
    have hsub0 : normSq - P.μ2 / (2 * P.𝓵) = 0 := sq_eq_zero_iff.mp hsq0
    linarith
  · intro hNorm
    rw [hNorm, sub_self]
    ring

end HiggsPotential

/-! ### 2. Bekenstein–Nyquist Saturation Functional & Sign-Convention Bridge -/

/-- Bekenstein–Nyquist Saturation Functional `Σ(F) = 1 - F / θ_Nyq`, where `θ_Nyq = Λ_Bek * ν_ML`. -/
def saturationFunctional (F θ_Nyq : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  1 - F / θ_Nyq

/-- Linear reduction of the active spatial Lax commutator defect `F(|c|²) = F₀ - κ |c|²`
    as ultraviolet norm `c_normSq = |c|²` is rotated into the worldline clock fiber. -/
def relaxedDefect (F₀ κ c_normSq : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  F₀ - κ * c_normSq

/-- Quartic self-coupling `λ = κ / (2 θ_Nyq)` induced by linear commutator relaxation. -/
def quarticCoupling (κ θ_Nyq : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  κ / (2 * θ_Nyq)

/-- **Linear Relaxation of the Saturation Functional (Monograph Eq. 19)**:
    Rotating norm `c_normSq = |c|²` into the clock fiber shifts the instantaneous
    saturation functional from `σ₀ = saturationFunctional F₀ θ_Nyq` to `σ₀ + 2 λ |c|²`. -/
theorem saturation_linear_relaxation (F₀ κ θ_Nyq c_normSq : ℝ) (hθ : θ_Nyq ≠ 0) :
    saturationFunctional (relaxedDefect F₀ κ c_normSq) θ_Nyq =
      saturationFunctional F₀ θ_Nyq + 2 * (quarticCoupling κ θ_Nyq) * c_normSq := by
  unfold saturationFunctional relaxedDefect quarticCoupling
  field_simp
  ring

/-- Effective Landau–Ginzburg–Higgs potential `V_eff(|c|²) = σ₀ |c|² + λ |c|⁴` (Monograph Eq. 20). -/
def nyquistEffectivePotential (σ₀ lam c_normSq : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  σ₀ * c_normSq + lam * c_normSq ^ 2

/-- Derivative of `nyquistEffectivePotential` with respect to `c_normSq = |c|²` recovers
    the relaxed saturation functional `Σ(|c|²) = σ₀ + 2 λ |c|²`. -/
def dV_dcNormSq (σ₀ lam c_normSq : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  σ₀ + 2 * lam * c_normSq

/-- Canonical bridge mapping Bekenstein–Nyquist parameters `(σ₀, lam)` to `Physlib`'s
    `HiggsPotential` via `μ2 := -σ₀` and `𝓵 := lam`. -/
def toHiggsPotential (σ₀ lam : ℝ) : HiggsPotential :=
  { μ2 := -σ₀, 𝓵 := lam }

/-- **Sign-Convention Bridge Identity**:
    `nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq` is identically equal to
    `(toHiggsPotential σ₀ lam).toFun c_normSq`. -/
theorem nyquistEffectivePotential_eq_higgsPotential (σ₀ lam c_normSq : ℝ) :
    nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq = (toHiggsPotential σ₀ lam).toFun c_normSq := by
  unfold nyquistEffectivePotential toHiggsPotential HiggsPotential.toFun
  ring

/-! ### 3. Horizon Criticality Duality Theorems (Elliptic Bulk vs. Hyperbolic Shock Core) -/

/-- **Sub-Nyquist Elliptic Bulk Unbroken Phase (`σ₀ > 0, λ > 0`)**:
    In the sub-Nyquist bulk where `σ₀ > 0`, `nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq ≥ 0`
    for all `c_normSq ≥ 0`, and vanishes if and only if `c_normSq = 0` (no clock condensation, `m_j = 0`). -/
theorem subNyquist_unique_minimum_zero
    (σ₀ lam c_normSq : ℝ) (hσ : 0 < σ₀) (hlam : 0 < lam) (hc : 0 ≤ c_normSq) :
    0 ≤ nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq ∧
      (nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq = 0 ↔ c_normSq = 0) := by
  unfold nyquistEffectivePotential
  have h1 : 0 ≤ σ₀ * c_normSq := mul_nonneg (le_of_lt hσ) hc
  have h2 : 0 ≤ lam * c_normSq ^ 2 := mul_nonneg (le_of_lt hlam) (sq_nonneg c_normSq)
  refine ⟨by linarith, ?_⟩
  constructor
  · intro hzero
    by_contra hne
    have hc_pos : 0 < c_normSq := lt_of_le_of_ne hc (Ne.symm hne)
    have h1_pos : 0 < σ₀ * c_normSq := mul_pos hσ hc_pos
    linarith
  · intro hc0
    rw [hc0]
    ring

/-- Equilibrium clock condensate norm squared `c_eq² = -σ₀ / (2λ)` in the super-Nyquist shock core. -/
def equilibriumClockNormSq (σ₀ lam : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  -σ₀ / (2 * lam)

/-- **Super-Nyquist Shock Core Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking (`σ₀ < 0, λ > 0`)**:
    When `σ₀ < 0` and `λ > 0`:
    1. The equilibrium clock condensate norm squared `equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam = -σ₀ / (2λ)` is strictly positive.
    2. For all `c_normSq`, `nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq ≥ - (σ₀² / (4λ))`.
    3. The global minimum `- (σ₀² / (4λ))` is attained if and only if `c_normSq = equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam`. -/
theorem superNyquist_condensate_minimum
    (σ₀ lam : ℝ) (hσ : σ₀ < 0) (hlam : 0 < lam) :
    0 < equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam ∧
    (∀ c_normSq : ℝ, - (σ₀ ^ 2 / (4 * lam)) ≤ nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq) ∧
    (∀ c_normSq : ℝ,
      nyquistEffectivePotential σ₀ lam c_normSq = - (σ₀ ^ 2 / (4 * lam)) ↔
        c_normSq = equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam) := by
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · unfold equilibriumClockNormSq
    have hneg : 0 < -σ₀ := neg_pos.mpr hσ
    have hden : 0 < 2 * lam := by linarith
    exact div_pos hneg hden
  · intro c_normSq
    rw [nyquistEffectivePotential_eq_higgsPotential]
    simpa [toHiggsPotential, neg_sq] using
      (toHiggsPotential σ₀ lam).pos_𝓵_lower_bound hlam c_normSq
  · intro c_normSq
    rw [nyquistEffectivePotential_eq_higgsPotential]
    simpa [toHiggsPotential, equilibriumClockNormSq, neg_sq] using
      (toHiggsPotential σ₀ lam).minimum_iff_normSq_eq hlam c_normSq

/-- **Sonic / Nyquist Horizon Restoration Identity (`Σ(|c_eq|²) = 0`)**:
    Condensing clock norm `c_eq² = equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam = -σ₀ / (2λ)` inside a
    super-Nyquist shock core (`σ₀ < 0`) drives the relaxed saturation functional
    `dV_dcNormSq σ₀ lam (equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam) = σ₀ + 2λ c_eq²` to zero,
    pinning the composite system back to the critical Sonic/Nyquist Horizon `Σ = 0`. -/
theorem superNyquist_horizon_restoration (σ₀ lam : ℝ) (hlam : lam ≠ 0) :
    dV_dcNormSq σ₀ lam (equilibriumClockNormSq σ₀ lam) = 0 := by
  unfold dV_dcNormSq equilibriumClockNormSq
  field_simp
  ring

/-- **Cutoff-Independent Higgs–Nyquist Stiffness (`O(Λ_Bek⁰)`)**:
    When the spillover absorption rate scales linearly with the Bekenstein cutoff (`κ = c₁ * Λ_Bek`)
    and the Nyquist threshold is `θ_Nyq = Λ_Bek * ν_ML` (`Λ_Bek ≠ 0`), the quartic coupling
    `quarticCoupling (c₁ * Λ_Bek) (Λ_Bek * ν_ML)` equals `c₁ / (2 * ν_ML)`, independent of `Λ_Bek`. -/
theorem quarticCoupling_cutoff_independent (c₁ Λ_Bek ν_ML : ℝ) (hΛ : Λ_Bek ≠ 0) :
    quarticCoupling (c₁ * Λ_Bek) (Λ_Bek * ν_ML) = c₁ / (2 * ν_ML) := by
  unfold quarticCoupling
  by_cases hν : ν_ML = 0
  · subst hν
    simp
  · field_simp

end BekensteinNyquist

import UnitaryBandwidthDepth
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.UnitaryGroup

/-!
# Bekenstein–Nyquist Gauge-Covariant Clock Transport & Cayley Unitarity (Phase A — Test A3)

This module formalizes **Test A3** of the Lean 4 Physics Extension Plan (`lean_physics_extension_plan.md`),
connecting the discrete 1+1D Unitary Nyquist Rotor Lattice (`toy_model_2_nyquist_rotor_pde.py` and
`toy_model_4_shock_collision_gate.py`) and Sections 2.1 and 4.1 of the monograph to formal
matrix algebra in Lean 4:

1. **Unconditional Unitarity of the Cayley / Crank–Nicolson Integrator (`cayleyTransform_mem_unitaryGroup`)**:
   Proves that for any skew-Hermitian operator `D` (`Dᴴ = -D`) and any left resolvent `R` of
   `1 + D` (`R * (1 + D) = 1`), the Cayley transform `U = R * (1 - D)`
   satisfies `U * Uᴴ = 1` and belongs to `Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ`.
2. **Skew-Hermiticity of Split-Form Burgers & Gauge-Covariant Clock Advection
   (`splitFormAdvection_skewHermitian`, `covariantClockAdvection_skewHermitian`)**:
   Proves that symmetric bond weights `w i j = w j i`, antisymmetric difference stencils
   `σ j i = -σ i j`, and Hermitian-oriented complex gauge links `U_link j i = star (U_link i j)`
   yield an identically skew-Hermitian generator `Dᴴ = -D`.
3. **Gauge Invariance of Worldline Wilson Holonomy & Two-Shock Ramsey Interference
   (`twoPathHolonomy_gauge_invariant`, `wilsonLoop3_gauge_invariant`,
   `covariantClockAdvection_gauge_equivariant`)**:
   Proves that under any local `U(1)` site gauge transformation `ψ : n → ℂ` with `ψ i * star (ψ i) = 1`,
   the covariant advection operator transforms equivariantly (`G D Gᴴ`) while the closed 2-branch
   Ramsey collision holonomy `U₁(i,k) * star (U₂(i,k))` and 3-site Wilson loop are strictly
   gauge-invariant.
-/

namespace BekensteinNyquist

open Matrix

/-- Complex conjugation fixes real scalars embedded in `ℂ`. -/
lemma star_ofReal_complex (r : ℝ) : star (r : ℂ) = (r : ℂ) :=
  Complex.conj_ofReal r

/-! ### 1. Cayley Transform Unitarity for Skew-Hermitian Generators -/

/-- The Cayley transform `U = (1 + D)⁻¹ (1 - D)` represented via an explicit resolvent
    matrix `R = (1 + D)⁻¹`, i.e., `cayleyTransform D R = R * (1 - D)`. -/
def cayleyTransform {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (D R : Matrix n n ℂ) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  R * (1 - D)

/-- For any matrix `D`, `1 + D` and `1 - D` commute: `(1 - D) * (1 + D) = (1 + D) * (1 - D)`. -/
lemma one_sub_mul_one_add_comm {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (D : Matrix n n ℂ) :
    (1 - D) * (1 + D) = (1 + D) * (1 - D) := by
  noncomm_ring

/-- For a skew-Hermitian matrix `D` (`Dᴴ = -D`), `(1 - D)ᴴ = 1 + D`. -/
lemma conjTranspose_one_sub_of_skewHermitian {n : Type*} [DecidableEq n]
    (D : Matrix n n ℂ) (hD : Dᴴ = -D) :
    (1 - D)ᴴ = 1 + D := by
  rw [conjTranspose_sub, conjTranspose_one, hD, sub_neg_eq_add]

/-- For a skew-Hermitian matrix `D` (`Dᴴ = -D`), `(1 + D)ᴴ = 1 - D`. -/
lemma conjTranspose_one_add_of_skewHermitian {n : Type*} [DecidableEq n]
    (D : Matrix n n ℂ) (hD : Dᴴ = -D) :
    (1 + D)ᴴ = 1 - D := by
  rw [conjTranspose_add, conjTranspose_one, hD, sub_eq_add_neg]

/-- If `R` is a left inverse of `1 + D` (`R * (1 + D) = 1`) and `D` is skew-Hermitian (`Dᴴ = -D`),
    then `Rᴴ` is a right inverse of `1 - D`: `(1 - D) * Rᴴ = 1`. -/
lemma one_sub_mul_conjTranspose_resolvent {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]
    (D R : Matrix n n ℂ) (hD : Dᴴ = -D) (hR_left : R * (1 + D) = 1) :
    (1 - D) * Rᴴ = 1 := by
  have h := congrArg conjTranspose hR_left
  rw [conjTranspose_mul, conjTranspose_one_add_of_skewHermitian D hD, conjTranspose_one] at h
  exact h

/-- **Exact Unitarity of the Cayley Transform (`U * Uᴴ = 1`)**:
    For any skew-Hermitian operator `D` (`Dᴴ = -D`) and resolvent `R` satisfying
    `R * (1 + D) = 1`, `cayleyTransform D R * (cayleyTransform D R)ᴴ = 1`. -/
theorem cayleyTransform_mul_conjTranspose_self {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]
    (D R : Matrix n n ℂ) (hD : Dᴴ = -D) (hR_left : R * (1 + D) = 1) :
    cayleyTransform D R * (cayleyTransform D R)ᴴ = 1 := by
  have hR_adj : (1 - D) * Rᴴ = 1 := one_sub_mul_conjTranspose_resolvent D R hD hR_left
  unfold cayleyTransform
  rw [conjTranspose_mul, conjTranspose_one_sub_of_skewHermitian D hD]
  calc
    R * (1 - D) * ((1 + D) * Rᴴ)
      = R * ((1 - D) * (1 + D)) * Rᴴ := by noncomm_ring
    _ = R * ((1 + D) * (1 - D)) * Rᴴ := by rw [one_sub_mul_one_add_comm D]
    _ = (R * (1 + D)) * ((1 - D) * Rᴴ) := by noncomm_ring
    _ = 1 * 1 := by rw [hR_left, hR_adj]
    _ = 1 := by noncomm_ring

/-- **Membership of the Cayley Transform in `Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ`**:
    Guarantees that the Crank–Nicolson / Cayley time-stepping operator
    `U(Δt) = (I + (Δt/2) D_w(u))⁻¹ (I - (Δt/2) D_w(u))` in Milestones 2 and 4 preserves the
    discrete L² norm identically for every velocity field `u` and step size `Δt`. -/
theorem cayleyTransform_mem_unitaryGroup {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]
    (D R : Matrix n n ℂ) (hD : Dᴴ = -D) (hR_left : R * (1 + D) = 1) :
    cayleyTransform D R ∈ Matrix.unitaryGroup n ℂ := by
  rw [Matrix.mem_unitaryGroup_iff]
  exact cayleyTransform_mul_conjTranspose_self D R hD hR_left

/-! ### 2. Skew-Hermiticity of Split-Form Burgers & Gauge-Covariant Clock Advection -/

/-- The discrete split-form Burgers advection matrix `D_{i,j} = w_{i,j} * σ_{i,j}`,
    where `w i j = (u_i + u_j)/3` is a symmetric bond weight and `σ i j` is an antisymmetric
    derivative stencil (`σ j i = -σ i j`). -/
def splitFormAdvection {n : Type*} (w σ : n → n → ℝ) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  Matrix.of (fun i j => ((w i j * σ i j : ℝ) : ℂ))

/-- **Skew-Hermiticity of the Split-Form Burgers Operator**:
    Whenever the velocity bond weight `w` is symmetric (`w i j = w j i`) and the finite-difference
    or spectral derivative kernel `σ` is antisymmetric (`σ j i = -σ i j`), the split-form
    advection matrix `splitFormAdvection w σ` satisfies `Dᴴ = -D`. -/
theorem splitFormAdvection_skewHermitian {n : Type*} (w σ : n → n → ℝ)
    (hw : ∀ i j, w i j = w j i) (hσ : ∀ i j, σ j i = -σ i j) :
    (splitFormAdvection w σ)ᴴ = -splitFormAdvection w σ := by
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.conjTranspose_apply, Matrix.neg_apply]
  simp only [splitFormAdvection, of_apply, star_ofReal_complex]
  rw [hw j i, hσ i j]
  push_cast
  ring

/-- The gauge-covariant clock parallel-transport advection matrix (Monograph Section 2.1):
    `D^cov_{i,j} = (w_{i,j} * σ_{i,j} : ℂ) * U_link i j`,
    where `U_link i j = exp(i θ_{i → j})` is the complex parallel-transport link phase along
    bond `(j → i)`. -/
def covariantClockAdvection {n : Type*} (w σ : n → n → ℝ) (U_link : n → n → ℂ) : Matrix n n ℂ :=
  Matrix.of (fun i j => ((w i j * σ i j : ℝ) : ℂ) * U_link i j)

/-- **Skew-Hermiticity of Gauge-Covariant Clock Advection**:
    For symmetric bond weights (`w i j = w j i`), antisymmetric derivative stencil (`σ j i = -σ i j`),
    and Hermitian-oriented gauge links (`U_link j i = star (U_link i j)`), the covariant
    clock transport generator satisfies `(covariantClockAdvection w σ U_link)ᴴ = -covariantClockAdvection w σ U_link`. -/
theorem covariantClockAdvection_skewHermitian {n : Type*} (w σ : n → n → ℝ) (U_link : n → n → ℂ)
    (hw : ∀ i j, w i j = w j i) (hσ : ∀ i j, σ j i = -σ i j)
    (hLink : ∀ i j, U_link j i = star (U_link i j)) :
    (covariantClockAdvection w σ U_link)ᴴ = -covariantClockAdvection w σ U_link := by
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.conjTranspose_apply, Matrix.neg_apply]
  simp only [covariantClockAdvection, of_apply, star_mul', star_ofReal_complex]
  rw [hw j i, hσ i j, hLink i j, star_star]
  push_cast
  ring

/-! ### 3. Gauge Invariance of Worldline Wilson Holonomy & Two-Shock Ramsey Interference -/

/-- Local `U(1)` gauge transformation of a lattice link variable `U_link i j` by site phases
    `ψ : n → ℂ`: `U'_{i,j} = ψ_i * U_{i,j} * star (ψ_j)`. -/
def gaugeTransformLink {n : Type*} (ψ : n → ℂ) (U_link : n → n → ℂ) (i j : n) : ℂ :=
  ψ i * U_link i j * star (ψ j)

/-- **Two-Shock Collision / Ramsey Interference Holonomy Gauge Invariance**:
    When two colliding shock worldlines propagate from source site `i` to collision site `k`
    along two branches with holonomies `U₁ i k` and `U₂ i k`, their Ramsey interference cross-term
    `U₁ i k * star (U₂ i k)` is strictly invariant under any local `U(1)` gauge transformation
    `ψ : n → ℂ` satisfying `ψ x * star (ψ x) = 1`. -/
theorem twoPathHolonomy_gauge_invariant {n : Type*} (ψ : n → ℂ) (U₁ U₂ : n → n → ℂ)
    (hψ : ∀ x, ψ x * star (ψ x) = 1) (i k : n) :
    gaugeTransformLink ψ U₁ i k * star (gaugeTransformLink ψ U₂ i k) =
      U₁ i k * star (U₂ i k) := by
  have hi : ψ i * star (ψ i) = 1 := hψ i
  have hk : ψ k * star (ψ k) = 1 := hψ k
  unfold gaugeTransformLink
  simp only [star_mul', star_star]
  calc
    ψ i * U₁ i k * star (ψ k) * (star (ψ i) * star (U₂ i k) * ψ k)
      = (ψ i * star (ψ i)) * (ψ k * star (ψ k)) * (U₁ i k * star (U₂ i k)) := by ring
    _ = 1 * 1 * (U₁ i k * star (U₂ i k)) := by rw [hi, hk]
    _ = U₁ i k * star (U₂ i k) := by ring

/-- **Closed 3-Site Wilson Loop Gauge Invariance**:
    For any closed 3-site plaquette `(i → j → k → i)`, the Wilson loop holonomy
    `U_{i,j} * U_{j,k} * U_{k,i}` is strictly invariant under any local `U(1)` gauge
    transformation `ψ : n → ℂ` satisfying `ψ x * star (ψ x) = 1`. -/
theorem wilsonLoop3_gauge_invariant {n : Type*} (ψ : n → ℂ) (U_link : n → n → ℂ)
    (hψ : ∀ x, ψ x * star (ψ x) = 1) (i j k : n) :
    gaugeTransformLink ψ U_link i j *
      gaugeTransformLink ψ U_link j k *
      gaugeTransformLink ψ U_link k i =
      U_link i j * U_link j k * U_link k i := by
  have hi : ψ i * star (ψ i) = 1 := hψ i
  have hj : ψ j * star (ψ j) = 1 := hψ j
  have hk : ψ k * star (ψ k) = 1 := hψ k
  unfold gaugeTransformLink
  calc
    ψ i * U_link i j * star (ψ j) * (ψ j * U_link j k * star (ψ k)) *
        (ψ k * U_link k i * star (ψ i))
      = (ψ i * star (ψ i)) * (ψ j * star (ψ j)) * (ψ k * star (ψ k)) *
          (U_link i j * U_link j k * U_link k i) := by ring
    _ = 1 * 1 * 1 * (U_link i j * U_link j k * U_link k i) := by rw [hi, hj, hk]
    _ = U_link i j * U_link j k * U_link k i := by ring

/-- Conjugate transpose of a diagonal complex matrix. -/
lemma conjTranspose_diagonal_complex {n : Type*} [DecidableEq n] (ψ : n → ℂ) :
    (Matrix.diagonal ψ)ᴴ = Matrix.diagonal (fun i => star (ψ i)) := by
  ext i j
  simp only [conjTranspose_apply, diagonal_apply]
  by_cases h : i = j
  · subst h; simp
  · have hji : j ≠ i := Ne.symm h
    simp [h, hji]

/-- **Gauge Equivariance of the Covariant Clock Advection Matrix**:
    Under a local site gauge transformation `U_link ↦ gaugeTransformLink ψ U_link`, the matrix
    `covariantClockAdvection w σ` transforms by unitary conjugation with `diagonal ψ`:
    `covariantClockAdvection w σ (gaugeTransformLink ψ U_link) = diagonal ψ * covariantClockAdvection w σ U_link * (diagonal ψ)ᴴ`. -/
theorem covariantClockAdvection_gauge_equivariant {n : Type*} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n]
    (w σ : n → n → ℝ) (U_link : n → n → ℂ) (ψ : n → ℂ) :
    covariantClockAdvection w σ (gaugeTransformLink ψ U_link) =
      Matrix.diagonal ψ * covariantClockAdvection w σ U_link * (Matrix.diagonal ψ)ᴴ := by
  ext i j
  rw [conjTranspose_diagonal_complex]
  simp only [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul, covariantClockAdvection,
    of_apply, gaugeTransformLink]
  ring

end BekensteinNyquist
